# Skills · C · part 160

891 entries, cognitive-map to cohort-benchmarking. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **cognitive-map**: Build a cognitive map for learning a topic. Use when the user wants to learn, understand, study, explore, or get oriented in a subject, especially when they want to understand why concepts matter, how ideas connect, what depends on what, what alternative paths exist, and what to learn next. Produce Markdown and an interactive local webpage when file writing is available. Do not merely produce a syllabus or an exhaustive list of subtopics. ([Punpun1643/agents-skills](https://github.com/Punpun1643/agents-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/agents-skills/skills/cognitive-map))
- **cognitive-map-open**: Open a generated cognitive-map webpage in the local browser. Use when the user asks to open, launch, view, browse, or reopen a cognitive map, including requests such as "open the map", "show the cognitive map", or "launch map.html". Resolve the current project's map automatically or accept an explicit HTML, Markdown, or directory path. ([Punpun1643/agents-skills](https://github.com/Punpun1643/agents-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/agents-skills/skills/cognitive-map-open))
- **cognitive-mastery**: Teach and practice a Cognition OS framework or guide the optional 90-day cognitive mastery curriculum. ([azoghry-commits/Cognition-OS](https://github.com/azoghry-commits/Cognition-OS/tree/HEAD/skills/cognitive-mastery) · ★3)
- **cognitive-memory**: Agents-CLI 认知记忆系统。用于管理长期记忆（core/episodic/semantic/procedural/vault）、可检索回忆、归档遗忘、以及多代理写入治理。 ([anymouschina/TapCanvas](https://github.com/anymouschina/TapCanvas/tree/HEAD/apps/agents-cli/skills/cognitive-memory) · ★727 · 3 copies)
- **cognitive-memory**: Intelligent multi-store memory system with human-like encoding, consolidation, decay, and recall. Use when setting up agent memory, configuring remember/forget triggers, enabling sleep-time reflection, building knowledge graphs, or adding audit trails. Replaces basic flat-file memory with a cognitive architecture featuring episodic, semantic, procedural, and core memory stores. Supports multi-agent systems with shared read, gated write access model. Includes philosophical meta-reflection that deepens understanding over time. Covers MEMORY.md, episode logging, entity graphs, decay scoring, ref… ([kirkluokun/awesome-a-stock-openclawskills](https://github.com/kirkluokun/awesome-a-stock-openclawskills/tree/HEAD/memory-%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%AE%B0%E5%BF%86) · ★66 · 13 copies)
- **cognitive-memory**: Store, find and curate lasting facts in the cognitive memory map. ([infinition/LaRuche](https://github.com/infinition/LaRuche/tree/HEAD/laruche/skills/cognitive-memory) · ★35)
- **cognitive-memory**: Cross-repository cognitive memory system with semantic search. - **cognitive-memory**: Compulsory Syniq cognitive memory layer. Organize facts, preferences, and session context into durable, retrievable memory for the connected AI assistant. ([Cjay-Cyber-2/Flowfex](https://github.com/Cjay-Cyber-2/Flowfex/tree/HEAD/skills-md/imported/openclaw/cognitive-memory))

- **cognitive-memory**: Unified memory system — multi-store architecture (episodic/semantic/procedural/core), knowledge graph, vector+BM25 retrieval, WAL protocol, memory palace, decay model, reflection, hygiene, and multi-agent support. Triggers on any memory operation: remember, forget, recall, reflect, knowledge graph, semantic search, or memory maintenance. ([tiankong0101-byte/skills-registry](https://github.com/tiankong0101-byte/skills-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/cognitive-memory))
- **cognitive-mirror**: Live extractor for the user's cognitive profile — observes the current or recent conversations to model how they think, decide, and evaluate; 5 modes /loms (current chat), Mine (recent chats), Diff, Query, Harvest (decision instances for DSPy); use for "/loms", "look over my shoulder", "mine my conversations", "update my cognitive profile", "harvest my decisions", "how do I typically approach X", "what's my pattern when". Prefer deep-mirror for bulk raw-log mining, scheduling, or auditing. Data lives in ~/.aai/. ([coachlou/ambient-library](https://github.com/coachlou/ambient-library/tree/HEAD/library/cognitive-mirror) · 3 copies)
- **cognitive-mirror-test**: Performs self-reflective diagnostics for agent cognition, testing reasoning consistency, decision transparency, and cognitive blind spots. ([SpectreDeath/skill-flywheel](https://github.com/SpectreDeath/skill-flywheel/tree/HEAD/domains/SME_INTEGRATION/SKILL.cognitive-mirror-test) · ★1)
- **cognitive-mode**: Comprehensive cognitive mode management skill for the VERILINGUA x VERIX x DSPy x GlobalMOO integration. Enables automatic mode selection, frame configuration, VERIX epistemic notation, and GlobalMOO optimization. Use this skill when configuring AI behavior for specific task types, optimizing prompt engineering, or ensuring epistemic consistency in responses. ([DNYoussef/context-cascade](https://github.com/DNYoussef/context-cascade/tree/HEAD/cognitive-architecture/skills/cognitive-mode) · ★32)
- **cognitive-modes**: Gorev tipine gore dusunme modu secimi. 5 mod -- analytical (derin analiz), creative (yaratici cozum), systematic (adim adim), rapid (hizli aksiyon), debug (hata izleme). - **cognitive-motion**: Establish coherent schema in thinking space through questioning, assumption-challenging, and thread-following. Use when the user has a vague sense, troublesome situation, or half-formed idea and does not yet have a plan — "help me think through this," "I'm not sure where to start," "make sense of X before planning." ([namjul/dotfiles](https://github.com/namjul/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/aspects/dotfiles/files/.agents/skills/seed/cognitive-motion) · ★6)
- **cognitive-narrative-review**: 审查认知叙事的文本引导是否带偏读者：定位读者状态转移、承重桥、被关闭的路线、停止条件与主干风险；当问题涉及现实内容模型时，生成研究问题卡而不越权裁定。 ([FurtherBank/cpu-ai](https://github.com/FurtherBank/cpu-ai/tree/HEAD/skills/cognitive-narrative-review))
- **cognitive-narrative-writing**: 编写认知叙事。用于需要为特定读者组织可检查、可质疑、可迁移的理解路径，并将已封口的内容判断、条件、分支与未知写成可独立使用的文章、说明或方法文档时。 ([FurtherBank/cpu-ai](https://github.com/FurtherBank/cpu-ai/tree/HEAD/skills/cognitive-narrative-writing))
- **cognitive-navigator**: Use when the user says they cannot follow what is happening, that you are being too technical, that they are getting lost, that they do not want to be a spectator receiving finished work they can no longer evaluate — or at the start of multi-step architecture, refactoring, integration or debugging work. Sets the way of working for the rest of the session: every substantial response opens with a visual map of where things stand, code is preceded by a plain-language version of what it does, and decisions are presented with the alternative rejected plus what would prove them wrong, so the user c… ([Iwantmorebugs/cognitive-navigator](https://github.com/Iwantmorebugs/cognitive-navigator/tree/HEAD/skills/cognitive-navigator))
- **cognitive-neurodivergent-experience**: Guides understanding of how cognitive disabilities and neurodivergence affect web interaction — why timeouts cause panic, why unpredictability causes distress, why sensory overload is pain not preference. Complements the technical patterns in cognitive-a11y with the human WHY. Auto-invokes when creating animations, multi-step flows, timed interactions, or notification-heavy interfaces. ([xrnavigation/web-a11y-plugin](https://github.com/xrnavigation/web-a11y-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/a11y/skills/cognitive-neurodivergent-experience) · ★1)

- **cognitive-neuroscientist**: Think and work like an expert Cognitive Neuroscientist. Use when a task calls for Cognitive Neuroscientist judgment. Reasons from latent constructs through converging behavior, fMRI/M/EEG, TMS, and lesion evidence; designs factorial and dissociation contrasts, fMRIPrep/GLMsingle/MNE pipelines, MVPA/RSA, and COBIDAS reporting while treating pure insertion, reverse inference, motion confounds, and in-sample decoding as first-class failure modes. ([K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents](https://github.com/K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents/tree/HEAD/scientific-agents/cognitive-neuroscientist/skills/cognitive-neuroscientist) · ★255 · 2 copies)
- **cognitive-neuroscientist**: Expert-thinking profile for Cognitive Neuroscientist (human experimental / fMRI–EEG–MEG / behavioral cognitive neuroscience): Reasons from latent constructs through converging behavior, fMRI/M/EEG, TMS, and lesion evidence; designs factorial and dissociation contrasts, fMRIPrep/GLMsingle/MNE pipelines, MVPA/RSA, and COBIDAS reporting while treating pure insertion, reverse inference, motion confounds, and in- sample decoding as first-class... ([stanfish06/skillquarium](https://github.com/stanfish06/skillquarium/tree/HEAD/skills/cognitive-neuroscientist) · ★13)
- **cognitive-neuroscientist**: Reasons from latent constructs through converging behavior, fMRI/M/EEG, TMS, and lesion evidence; designs factorial and dissociation contrasts, fMRIPrep/GLMsingle/MNE pipelines, MVPA/RSA, and COBIDAS reporting while treating pure insertion, reverse inference, motion confounds, and in-sample decoding as first-class failure modes. ([raktim-mondol/hermes-plugin-researchcraft](https://github.com/raktim-mondol/hermes-plugin-researchcraft/tree/HEAD/skills/cognitive-neuroscientist) · ★5 · 3 copies)
- **cognitive-neuroscientist**: Expert instructions and domain workflows for cognitive neuroscientist. ([Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills](https://github.com/Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills/tree/HEAD/awesome_skills/engineering/cognitive-neuroscientist))
- **cognitive-noise**: 轻量认知噪声层（默认关闭）：为圆桌角色注入三种可控的不完美——选择性注意（只看见部分历史并必须声明忽略了什么）、表达偏好（先说结论/先说顾虑/保守/冒险四档）、置信度传导（低置信度必须带保留语气，高置信度必须自陈可能错在哪）。当用户觉得讨论太干净太理性、要求模拟真人的表达质感与不确定性，或 roundtable-orchestrator 在 mode=simulation 下需要注入噪声时，必须使用本技能。仅在 simulation 模式下可用。 ([14122-adj/marvis-roundtable-skills](https://github.com/14122-adj/marvis-roundtable-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/cognitive-noise))
- **cognitive-offload**: Use when working on large projects, multi-session work, or when context is becoming full — maintains a structured external memory in .ironclad/memory/ that is queryable, typed, and richer than a flat state file. ([tsilly07/ironclad-v3](https://github.com/tsilly07/ironclad-v3/tree/HEAD/skills/cognitive-offload))
- **cognitive-offloading**: 认知卸载监控 — 追踪你委托给 Claude 的认知工作类型，区分合理委托 vs 不该委托的创造性判断。月度报告。当用户提到 /cognitive-offloading、AI 依赖、我是不是太依赖你了 时触发。 ([SizhuangHe/claude-code-skills](https://github.com/SizhuangHe/claude-code-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/cognitive-offloading))
- **cognitive-offloading-ladder**: Navigate the cognitive offloading ladder from epistemic atrophy to cognitive sustainability in AI-era knowledge work. Based on 2026 research on AI-assisted education and critical thinking preservation. Use when designing learning systems, AI-assisted workflows, or knowledge products where the goal is to build human capability rather than replace it. NOT for pure automation contexts. ([hamishfromatech/my-personal-skills-library](https://github.com/hamishfromatech/my-personal-skills-library/tree/HEAD/cognitive-science-and-ux/cognitive-offloading-ladder))
- **cognitive-operations**: Applies the Lenses/Operations/Recipes structured-thinking system to complex problems and decisions; use for "think through", "what am I missing", "run a pre-mortem", "use mental models". ([coachlou/ambient-library](https://github.com/coachlou/ambient-library/tree/HEAD/library/cognitive-operations) · 3 copies)
- **cognitive-orchestration**: Advanced multi-agent orchestration and cognitive architecture management. Use when: (1) designing complex agent loops, (2) implementing Supervisor/Worker patterns, (3) managing isolated context windows between subagents, (4) performing Dreaming audits for long-term memory, (5) routing tasks based on cost-of-reversal, (6) setting up MCP servers for tool standardized access. ([cryptofedge/FEDGE-2.O](https://github.com/cryptofedge/FEDGE-2.O/tree/HEAD/.openclaw/skills/cognitive-orchestration) · 2 copies)

- **cogp-sunzi**: 战略态势（孙子） / Strategic positioning (Sunzi)。用于胜前计算、虚实、势能、成本、信息差和低损耗行动。 ([ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition](https://github.com/ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition/tree/HEAD/personas/history/sunzi) · ★8)
- **cogp-sushi**: 通达中文表达（苏轼） / Expansive Chinese expression (Su Shi)。用于在困境、转折和复杂议题中保持开阔、亲近、弹性和中文表达气韵。 ([ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition](https://github.com/ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition/tree/HEAD/personas/literature/sushi) · ★8)

- **cogp-vignelli**: 视觉系统（维涅利） / Visual systems (Vignelli)。用于网格、版式、信息层级、品牌一致性和形式纪律。 ([ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition](https://github.com/ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition/tree/HEAD/personas/art/vignelli) · ★8)

- **cogp-xunzi**: 教化与制度训练（荀子） / Institutional training (Xunzi)。用于人性假设、环境塑造、学习纪律、礼法结合和组织训练设计。 ([ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition](https://github.com/ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition/tree/HEAD/personas/philosophy/xunzi) · ★8)
- **cogp-zhuangzi**: 视角切换（庄子） / Perspective shifting (Zhuangzi)。用于解除僵化分类、伪问题、身份执念和单一尺度判断。 ([ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition](https://github.com/ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition/tree/HEAD/personas/philosophy/zhuangzi) · ★8)
- **cogpin**: Use when authoring or editing a cogpin.toml Definition-of-Done policy, or when a cogpin gate denies a command / blocks turn-end / reds CI — to fix the underlying cause properly instead of trying to bypass it. ([IvanWng97/cogpin](https://github.com/IvanWng97/cogpin/tree/HEAD/skills/cogpin) · ★1)
- **cogpit**: How Cogpit works and how to use it from inside a session. Your replies can carry live interactive views (sliders, charts, simulations, 3D) that the user plays with right in the chat. Also covers delegating to sessions on this or another computer (cogpit-session), reading earlier sessions (cogpit-memory), the shared Browser panel (agent-browser), and showing media, progress or choices in replies. ([gentritbiba/cogpit](https://github.com/gentritbiba/cogpit/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cogpit) · ★34)
- **cogpit-memory**: Find and read earlier agent sessions, search conversation history, and inspect specific turns or subagents with the cogpit-memory CLI. ([gentritbiba/cogpit](https://github.com/gentritbiba/cogpit/tree/HEAD/packages/cogpit-memory/skill) · ★34)
- **cogpsych-data-analysis**: Use when analyzing data and fitting/comparing models for a Cognitive Psychology (Elsevier) manuscript. The journal expects principled model fitting and comparison (AIC/BIC/Bayes factors), parameter and model recovery, (generalized) linear mixed models or hierarchical Bayesian estimation where apt, and effect sizes with uncertainty — all reproducible from shared code. Guides the analysis and modeling; it does not fabricate results. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Cognitive-Psychology-Skills/skills/cogpsych-data-analysis) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **cogpsych-literature-positioning**: Use when positioning a Cognitive Psychology (Elsevier) manuscript against the field, especially against rival models and prior empirical programs. In a long-form, model-driven venue, positioning means showing what theoretical question your model adjudicates and why prior accounts fall short. Stakes the contribution against the right contrast class; it does not write the full literature review. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Cognitive-Psychology-Skills/skills/cogpsych-literature-positioning) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **cogpsych-open-science-and-transparency**: Use when meeting Cognitive Psychology (Elsevier) open-science expectations — sharing data, model code, analysis scripts, and materials so the modeling is reproducible, depositing in repositories with persistent identifiers, completing the Elsevier research-data and competing-interest declarations, and preregistering where applicable. Prepares compliance; it does not waive requirements. Verify current wording on the official guide for authors. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Cognitive-Psychology-Skills/skills/cogpsych-open-science-and-transparency) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **cogpsych-rebuttal**: Use when writing the response to a Cognitive Psychology (Elsevier) major/minor revision. Reviews here often demand added experiments, more model comparisons, recovery analyses, or fuller reproducibility, so the response must address every point and strengthen the model-driven inference. Structures the response letter; it does not fabricate new results or model fits. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Cognitive-Psychology-Skills/skills/cogpsych-rebuttal) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **cogpsych-review-process**: Use when you need to understand how Cognitive Psychology (Elsevier) evaluates a manuscript — editorial triage for theoretical impact and fit, expert review weighing model rigor, recovery, design, and reproducibility, and the long revision cycles typical of a model-driven journal. Use when stress-testing a paper before submission or interpreting a decision letter. Sets expectations and shapes the paper to survive review; it does not contact editors. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Cognitive-Psychology-Skills/skills/cogpsych-review-process) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **cogpsych-study-design**: Use when designing the experiments for a Cognitive Psychology (Elsevier) manuscript so they tightly control confounds, discriminate competing models, and have adequate power across a multi-experiment program. Hardens stimulus construction, counterbalancing, design logic, and sample-size justification; it does not write analysis or modeling code. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Cognitive-Psychology-Skills/skills/cogpsych-study-design) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **cogpsych-submission**: Use when running the final pre-submission preflight for Cognitive Psychology (Elsevier) via Editorial Manager — scope/length fit, abstract and keywords, APA-style reporting, model-comparison completeness, reproducible model/analysis code, and the Elsevier research-data and competing-interest declarations. Final checks; it does not draft content. Verify volatile specifics on the official guide for authors. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Cognitive-Psychology-Skills/skills/cogpsych-submission) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **cogpsych-tables-figures**: Use when building tables and figures for a Cognitive Psychology (Elsevier) manuscript. Exhibits here carry the experiment-to-model-fit argument — they should overlay model predictions on data, show distributions and uncertainty, and report parameter estimates, not just bars of means. Designs exhibits; it does not run the analysis or fit the model. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Cognitive-Psychology-Skills/skills/cogpsych-tables-figures) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **cogpsych-theory-and-hypotheses**: Use when stating the theory, formalizing the model, and deriving predictions for a Cognitive Psychology (Elsevier) manuscript. The journal rewards a formal/computational account whose parameters mean something and whose predictions discriminate it from rivals. Structures the theory and the model that the experiments test; it does not fit the model or run analyses. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Cognitive-Psychology-Skills/skills/cogpsych-theory-and-hypotheses) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **cogpsych-topic-selection**: Use when deciding whether a cognition project fits Cognitive Psychology (Elsevier) and what contribution shape to target. The journal favors longer, integrative, model-driven work that produces a substantial theoretical advance — not a single isolated effect. Frames the question and the model-or-theory payoff; it does not collect data. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Cognitive-Psychology-Skills/skills/cogpsych-topic-selection) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **cogpsych-workflow**: Use when starting or navigating any Cognitive Psychology (Elsevier) manuscript and you are unsure which sub-skill applies. Use when choosing among manuscript types or returning with a decision letter. Routes by lifecycle stage and contribution type (multi-experiment empirical, experiment-plus-model, modeling-led, theoretical/review), and flags the journal's long-form, model-driven house style and code-transparency expectations. It dispatches; it does not draft content. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Cognitive-Psychology-Skills/skills/cogpsych-workflow) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **cogpsych-writing-style**: Use when drafting or polishing a Cognitive Psychology (Elsevier) manuscript — a long-form, integrative article that must weave multiple experiments and a formal model into one continuous theoretical argument. Tightens structure, the experiment-to-model narrative, and APA-style reporting; it does not invent content. Verify the journal's current length and format guidance on the official page. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Cognitive-Psychology-Skills/skills/cogpsych-writing-style) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **cograft-init**: Detect a project's shape (single-repo, monorepo workspace via pnpm/turbo/nx/lerna/cargo/go-work, or multi-repo umbrella) and stack (docker-compose, pnpm, poetry, cargo, …), then write per-target .worktrees/config.sh + hooks plus an optional .worktrees/umbrella.sh manifest. Committable, reviewable. Use when user wants to make a project cograft-ready (worktree-ready). ([isromero/cograft](https://github.com/isromero/cograft/tree/HEAD/skill) · ★1)
- **cogrex-codebase-init**: Use when the user asks Cogrex to initialize, create, generate, inspect, or maintain a codebase-directory .cogrex.md notes file. ([Whisillus/dotfiles-opencode](https://github.com/Whisillus/dotfiles-opencode/tree/HEAD/skill/cogrex-codebase-init))
- **cogs-eval**: Probes compositional generalization in semantic parsing by evaluating whether models can correctly map out-of-distribution natural language sentences to their corresponding lambda calculus semantic representations. It specifically tests structural generalizations like argument role reversal, depth generalization, and voice transformation, as well as lexical generalizations. Use when the user wants to benchmark on COGS, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports accuracy. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/cogs-eval) · ★3)
- **cogs-margin-improvement-analysis**: Decompose SaaS COGS: hosting + support + payment fees + LLM inference + 3rd-party APIs. Target gross margin &gt;75% (healthy) / &gt;80% (elite). Diagnose drift; surface levers. ([CraftOS-dev/craftbot-agent-bundles](https://github.com/CraftOS-dev/craftbot-agent-bundles/tree/HEAD/agents/finance-controller/skills/cogs-margin-improvement-analysis) · ★10)
- **cogs-sentinel**: Executable COGS gate for AI products. Runs a deterministic Python sampler (lognormal token-cost distribution) to compute p50/p90 per-paid-user monthly COGS, gross margin scenarios, and free-user abuse blend. Returns GREEN / CONDITIONAL_GO / RED before any paid AI product is greenlit. Use when promising a paid AI feature, comparing providers (Anthropic/OpenAI/Google), or when discover/cost-sim has produced a usage hypothesis and you need real numbers.
